Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The p-adic operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 244. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 202 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_244(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 244. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^202(S, F) is trivial. QED.
